[01:20:32 INF] Bootstrapping RLS infrastructure...
[01:20:32 INF] Executed DbCommand (1ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='Text', CommandTimeout='30']
-- =============================================================================
-- Karamchari RLS bootstrap, step 0: shared 'security' schema.
--
-- Runs ONCE per database. Owns the predicate function and the per-tenant
-- security policies. Lives outside any tenant schema because it must be visible
-- to every tenant table without being subject to schema rewriting.
-- =============================================================================

IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.schemas WHERE name = N'security')
BEGIN
    EXEC(N'CREATE SCHEMA [security] AUTHORIZATION [dbo];');
END
[01:20:32 INF] Executed DbCommand (7ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='Text', CommandTimeout='30']
-- =============================================================================
-- Karamchari RLS bootstrap, step 1: tenant access predicate function.
--
-- Single shared predicate, parameterized by the row's TenantId. The session
-- value is set by RlsSessionContextInterceptor on every connection open.
--
-- Behaviour:
--   - Returns 1 row only when @TenantId equals SESSION_CONTEXT('TenantId').
--   - When SESSION_CONTEXT is not set, the comparison evaluates to UNKNOWN
--     and the row is filtered out (fail-closed).
--   - There is intentionally NO admin / db_owner escape hatch here. Cross-tenant
--     work (e.g. the outbox relay) MUST iterate tenants and set
--     SESSION_CONTEXT for each tenant in turn.
--
-- WITH SCHEMABINDING is required for the function to be usable as a security
-- policy predicate.
--
-- IDEMPOTENCY: This function is referenced by every per-tenant security policy.
-- Once any policy binds to it the function CANNOT be dropped or altered while
-- referenced (SQL error 3729). It is therefore created ONLY IF MISSING and is
-- never dropped here. Its definition is stable; if it ever must change, the
-- dependent per-tenant policies are dropped first by 02_tenant_policy. This
-- makes the bootstrap safe to re-run on every deploy.
-- =============================================================================

IF OBJECT_ID(N'[security].[fn_tenant_access]', N'IF') IS NULL
BEGIN
    EXEC(N'
        CREATE FUNCTION [security].[fn_tenant_access](@TenantId NVARCHAR(64))
        RETURNS TABLE
        WITH SCHEMABINDING
        AS
        RETURN
            SELECT 1 AS fn_result
            WHERE @TenantId IS NOT NULL
              AND @TenantId = CAST(SESSION_CONTEXT(N''TenantId'') AS NVARCHAR(64));
    ');
END

-- =============================================================================
-- Karamchari RLS, step 2: per-tenant security policy.
--
-- Template. Tokens substituted by RlsScriptGenerator at provisioning time:
--   TenantPolicy_dev    e.g.  TenantPolicy_acme
--   {SchemaName}    e.g.  tenant_acme
--   TableList         repeated FILTER + BLOCK ADD lines, generated per registered table
--
-- A policy holds many predicates and is created/altered atomically. We emit:
--   FILTER PREDICATE   — protects SELECT (visible rows must match TenantId)
--   BLOCK  PREDICATE   AFTER  INSERT  — INSERT'd row must match TenantId
--   BLOCK  PREDICATE   BEFORE UPDATE  — current row's TenantId must match
--   BLOCK  PREDICATE   AFTER  UPDATE  — updated row's TenantId must still match
--   BLOCK  PREDICATE   BEFORE DELETE  — current row's TenantId must match
--
-- The result: even raw ADO.NET that bypasses the schema interceptor cannot
-- read or write data belonging to another tenant.
--
-- Idempotent: drops the policy if it already exists, then recreates it.
-- =============================================================================

IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM sys.security_policies
    WHERE name = N'TenantPolicy_dev' AND schema_id = SCHEMA_ID(N'security')
)
BEGIN
    EXEC(N'DROP SECURITY POLICY [security].[TenantPolicy_dev];');
END
[01:20:32 INF] Executed DbCommand (2ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='Text', CommandTimeout='30']
CREATE SECURITY POLICY [security].[TenantPolicy_dev]
WITH (STATE = ON, SCHEMABINDING = ON);
[01:20:33 INF] Executed DbCommand (342ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='Text', CommandTimeout='30']
IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.columns WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID('[tenant_dev].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions]') AND name = 'TenantId')
BEGIN
    EXEC('ALTER SECURITY POLICY [security].[TenantPolicy_dev] ADD FILTER PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_dev].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions],
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_dev].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions] AFTER INSERT,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_dev].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions] BEFORE UPDATE,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_dev].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions] AFTER UPDATE,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_dev].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions] BEFORE DELETE');
END

[01:20:33 INF] Executed DbCommand (50ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='Text', CommandTimeout='30']
-- =============================================================================
-- Karamchari RLS, step 2: per-tenant security policy.
--
-- Template. Tokens substituted by RlsScriptGenerator at provisioning time:
--   TenantPolicy_acme    e.g.  TenantPolicy_acme
--   {SchemaName}    e.g.  tenant_acme
--   TableList         repeated FILTER + BLOCK ADD lines, generated per registered table
--
-- A policy holds many predicates and is created/altered atomically. We emit:
--   FILTER PREDICATE   — protects SELECT (visible rows must match TenantId)
--   BLOCK  PREDICATE   AFTER  INSERT  — INSERT'd row must match TenantId
--   BLOCK  PREDICATE   BEFORE UPDATE  — current row's TenantId must match
--   BLOCK  PREDICATE   AFTER  UPDATE  — updated row's TenantId must still match
--   BLOCK  PREDICATE   BEFORE DELETE  — current row's TenantId must match
--
-- The result: even raw ADO.NET that bypasses the schema interceptor cannot
-- read or write data belonging to another tenant.
--
-- Idempotent: drops the policy if it already exists, then recreates it.
-- =============================================================================

IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM sys.security_policies
    WHERE name = N'TenantPolicy_acme' AND schema_id = SCHEMA_ID(N'security')
)
BEGIN
    EXEC(N'DROP SECURITY POLICY [security].[TenantPolicy_acme];');
END
[01:20:33 INF] Executed DbCommand (3ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='Text', CommandTimeout='30']
CREATE SECURITY POLICY [security].[TenantPolicy_acme]
WITH (STATE = ON, SCHEMABINDING = ON);
[01:20:33 INF] Executed DbCommand (429ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='Text', CommandTimeout='30']
IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.columns WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID('[tenant_acme].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions]') AND name = 'TenantId')
BEGIN
    EXEC('ALTER SECURITY POLICY [security].[TenantPolicy_acme] ADD FILTER PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_acme].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions],
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_acme].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions] AFTER INSERT,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_acme].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions] BEFORE UPDATE,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_acme].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions] AFTER UPDATE,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_acme].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions] BEFORE DELETE');
END

    EXEC('ALTER SECURITY POLICY [security].[TenantPolicy_acme] ADD FILTER PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_acme].[Billing_BillableEntries],
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_acme].[Billing_BillableEntries] AFTER INSERT,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_acme].[Billing_BillableEntries] BEFORE UPDATE,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_acme].[Billing_BillableEntries] AFTER UPDATE,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_acme].[Billing_BillableEntries] BEFORE DELETE');

    EXEC('ALTER SECURITY POLICY [security].[TenantPolicy_acme] ADD FILTER PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_acme].[PayrollCorrectionStates],
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_acme].[PayrollCorrectionStates] AFTER INSERT,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_acme].[PayrollCorrectionStates] BEFORE UPDATE,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_acme].[PayrollCorrectionStates] AFTER UPDATE,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_acme].[PayrollCorrectionStates] BEFORE DELETE');
END

[01:20:33 INF] Executed DbCommand (47ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='Text', CommandTimeout='30']
-- =============================================================================
-- Karamchari RLS, step 2: per-tenant security policy.
--
-- Template. Tokens substituted by RlsScriptGenerator at provisioning time:
--   TenantPolicy_contoso    e.g.  TenantPolicy_acme
--   {SchemaName}    e.g.  tenant_acme
--   TableList         repeated FILTER + BLOCK ADD lines, generated per registered table
--
-- A policy holds many predicates and is created/altered atomically. We emit:
--   FILTER PREDICATE   — protects SELECT (visible rows must match TenantId)
--   BLOCK  PREDICATE   AFTER  INSERT  — INSERT'd row must match TenantId
--   BLOCK  PREDICATE   BEFORE UPDATE  — current row's TenantId must match
--   BLOCK  PREDICATE   AFTER  UPDATE  — updated row's TenantId must still match
--   BLOCK  PREDICATE   BEFORE DELETE  — current row's TenantId must match
--
-- The result: even raw ADO.NET that bypasses the schema interceptor cannot
-- read or write data belonging to another tenant.
--
-- Idempotent: drops the policy if it already exists, then recreates it.
-- =============================================================================

IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM sys.security_policies
    WHERE name = N'TenantPolicy_contoso' AND schema_id = SCHEMA_ID(N'security')
)
BEGIN
    EXEC(N'DROP SECURITY POLICY [security].[TenantPolicy_contoso];');
END
[01:20:33 INF] Executed DbCommand (2ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='Text', CommandTimeout='30']
CREATE SECURITY POLICY [security].[TenantPolicy_contoso]
WITH (STATE = ON, SCHEMABINDING = ON);
[01:20:34 INF] Executed DbCommand (395ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='Text', CommandTimeout='30']
IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.columns WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID('[tenant_contoso].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions]') AND name = 'TenantId')
BEGIN
    EXEC('ALTER SECURITY POLICY [security].[TenantPolicy_contoso] ADD FILTER PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_contoso].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions],
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_contoso].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions] AFTER INSERT,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_contoso].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions] BEFORE UPDATE,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_contoso].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions] AFTER UPDATE,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_contoso].[Workforce_AttendanceSessions] BEFORE DELETE');
END

    EXEC('ALTER SECURITY POLICY [security].[TenantPolicy_contoso] ADD FILTER PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_contoso].[KPISnapshots],
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_contoso].[KPISnapshots] AFTER INSERT,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_contoso].[KPISnapshots] BEFORE UPDATE,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_contoso].[KPISnapshots] AFTER UPDATE,
        ADD BLOCK  PREDICATE [security].[fn_tenant_access]([TenantId]) ON [tenant_contoso].[KPISnapshots] BEFORE DELETE');
END
